Potential algorithm: "Frank-Wolfe with active LP" might be powerful. Let's formalize the fixed-point linearization:
Given a (sum1, nonneg). Solve LP:
minimize t
subject conv(a,b)=d <= t (elementwise)
sum b=1, b>=0.
This finds b that minimizes maximum correlation with fixed a. It's a linear program with n variables and 2n-1 constraints. For n=3809, using cvxpy with SCS may be slow but maybe okay every few seconds. Then update b. We can also solve dual to get active set. This is analogous to "best response" in a zero-sum game? Algorithm for one sequence length:
- Initialize a as sum-normalized nonnegative smooth random: e.g., start from constant, add small sinusoidal antisymmetric plus symmetric modes, or sample from a log-normal smooth noise, clip.
- For several epochs, reduce temperature τ. At each step:
  1. Ensure a nonneg sum1 (project).
  2. C = ifft(rfft(a)^2) zero-padded length 2N-1.
  3. Compute weights w = softmax(C/τ) (with max subtract). τ maybe scaled relative to C range. Since C values ~1/N. For maximizing, use τ = k * std(C) or median. LSE approximates max when τ small.
  4. Gradient sg = cross_correlation(w, a): for each j, sum_m w_m(a[m-j]+a[j-m]). Compute via FFT:
    Need convolve w (length 2N-1) with reversed a? Let's derive efficient.
    sg[j] = Σ_m w_m a[m-j] + Σ_m w_m a[j-m].
    Let A0[j]=a[j]. Let W length L=2N-1.
    Term1[j] = Σ_m W[m] A0[m-j] where A0 out-of-bounds=0. This is cross-correlation of W and a: (W ⋆ a)[j] = Σ_m W[m] a[m-j]. This can be computed as ifft(fft(W, size) * conj(fft(a, size)))? Need indexing. For real signals, numpy correlation via convolution: corr[j] = ifft(fft(W) * conj(fft(a))) gives circular correlation: Σ_m W[(m+j) mod size] a[m]? We can avoid by using convolution with reversed: corr = convolve(W, flip(a))? Let's test concept. Let A_pad length size, W_pad. Linear convolution R = convolve(W, flip(a)) of length L+N-1. R[k] = Σ_i W[i] a[j]? If flip(a) index r = N-1-j. R[k] = Σ_i W[i] flipa[k-i]=Σ_i W[i] a[N-1-(k-i)] = Σ_i W[i] a[i+k-N+1]. We want Term1[j]=Σ_m W[m] a[m-j]. Set i=m, need index i+k-N+1 = i-j => k = N-1 - j. So Term1[j] = R[N-1-j]. Good.
    Term2[j]=Σ_m W[m] a[j-m] = Σ_m W[m] a[(j)-m]. This is similar correlation with lag -j: R2? It's Term1[-j]? But j nonnegative. Could compute as Term1b[j] = Σ_m W[m] a[j-m]. Let a index = j-m. Set R as above: index = i+k-N+1 = j - i => k = N-1 + j. Then Term2[j] = R[N-1+j]. So sg = R[N-1-j] + R[N-1+j] for j=0..N-1. Nice. Only one linear convolution of W (length L) and flip(a) (length N): size L+N-1 = 3N-2. Use FFT length next pow2 ~ 3N. For N=3809, 8192? 3N=11427, next pow2 16384. One rfft each of W and flipa per step. But W depends on C; C uses fft(a). We can maybe compute sg using weights from C and a; two FFTs: one for C (a^2) and one for W with a. Actually C = ifft(rfft(a)^2). Need rfft(a). Then R = ifft(rfft(W)*rfft(flipa)). This is 3 rffts. Each step maybe 1ms? For tens thousands okay.
  5. Update a. Since objective invariant to scaling and constraint sum nonnegative, we can do a predicted: a = a - lr*sg; project to simplex or multiply by positive. Projected gradient: need maintain sum=1. Use projection to probability simplex (a >=0, sum=1) for accurate. Projection O(N log N) each step may be okay for 20k steps but maybe slow. Alternative: center sg (sg - mean(sg)) so sum unchanged approximately, take step, then clip negatives and renormalize: a = np.clip(a - lr*sg_center,0,None); a/=a.sum(). This is simpler and often fine. Use Adam for adaptive per coordinate.
  6. Track best score via internal M.
  7. Temperature schedule: start τ large (e.g., 0.5*range(C)) to explore full shape, reduce gradually. Since C scales ~1/N, use normalized z=C/M? Could set τ = alpha * max(C) / ? For LSE of C, to approximate max with weights over 2N values, choose τ ~ (maxC - meanC)/5? But C values all similar; at target peak/avg 1.5, range about avg*0.5. We can set temperature = max(1e-9, tau_factor * (C.max()-C.min()))? If C flat, range small, temperature small; but then weights huge. Maybe use normalized C by S² (S=1) and target scale? Better use C * (2N) (so average ~ tan? Actually 2N*C ~ M*2N? Score/avg?). Let F=2N*C, average ~2, peak ~target~1.5? Wait if C average=1/(2N), then 2N*C average=1. Constant max=2, target 1.5. Nice! Work with D = 2N*C. Then objective = max D. Average D=1. This is scale-independent. Temperature for LSE around D. Start τ = 1.0 (or 0.5), reduce to 0.05 maybe. LSE approximate max. Gradient of LSE(D) w.r.t a includes factor 1/τ. We can ignore constant scaling by using weights softmax(D/τ). If τ small, weights near max.
  8. To avoid convergence to bad local, use multiple τ and restarts.

```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    a=a/s
    return a
```

D=C*(2*n)  # S=1 avg 1 maybe small variations
# weights = softmax(D/tau)
z=D/tau
z=z-z.max()
w=np.exp(z)
w/=w.sum()
size=1
while size<3*n-2: size*=2
fw=np.fft.fft(w, size)
fa=np.fft.fft(a, size)
cplus=np.fft.ifft(fw*np.conj(fa), size)[:n].real
cminus=np.fft.ifft(fw*fa, size)[:n].real
g=cplus+cminus
g-=g.mean()
return g
```

Optimizer details for one restart:
```
def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, seed, tau_start=0.8, tau_min=0.03, max_iter=50000):
    rng=...
    a=normalize(a0)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score(a)
    lr=0.3  # relative move fraction of mean
    beta=0.8
    vel=zeros
    no_accept=0
    for it ...:
        C=conv2(a); cur=score_from(C)
        if cur<best: best...
        D=C*(len(a))
        # tau anneal
        tau = max(tau_min, tau_start * (tau_min/tau_start)**(it/anneal_iters))
        w=softmax(D/tau)
        g=gradient_weights(w,a)
        g -= g.mean()
        gvar=np.std(g)
        if gvar<1e-15: break
        g/=gvar
        # add smoothness gradient (optional)
        g_smooth = 2*a - roll(a,1)-roll(a,-1)
        g_smooth /= (np.std(a)+1e-12) maybe
        g = g + 0.02*g_smooth
        vel = beta*vel + g
        move = lr * np.mean(a)
        a_try = a + move*vel
        a_try = normalize(a_try)
        # maybe blur? a_try = (1-smooth)*a_try + smooth*average over small window
        trial_C=conv2(a_try); trial_score=score_from
        if finite and trial_score < cur:
            a=a_try; cur=trial_score; lr=min(2.0, lr*1.03); no_accept=0
        else:
            lr *= 0.7
            no_accept +=1
            if no_accept > 50:
                # local perturbation
                a = normalize(a + rng.normal(0, 0.02*np.mean(a), n)) #? This may be too small? Use block
                vel*=0.5; no_accept=0
            if lr < 0.01:
                # reinitialize? return
                break
    return best,best_score
```
Move `lr * mean(a)`: if lr=0.3, move=0.000075 for n=4096 (mean=0.000244), vel standardized ~1, so 30% of mean entry. Good. Clipping may zero some. If lr grows to2, move 0.0005 ~2 entry, can create spikes. Maybe cap lr=1.0.

Gradient sign: We want reduce peaks. For a single active m, g[j] high where a[j] is near a[?]. Update a += move*vel (since vel=g centered). We should subtract where g high. So `a_try = a - move*vel` (if g high -> reduce). In code above I used +. Need choose. Let's set `a_try = a - move*vel`. Since g centered mean0, subtracting high g lowers them, raises negative g. Good.

Smoothness gradient sign: To smooth, subtract gradient of diff energy: g_smooth = 2a - neighbors. Where a high peak, g_smooth positive, subtract -> lower; in valleys negative, subtract negative -> raise. So add to g before subtract. Good.

But if g normalized, adding g_smooth may dominate. Use small coefficient and also normalize g_smooth: `g_smooth = 2a - np.roll(a,1)-np.roll(a,-1); gsmooth_var=np.std(g_smooth); if >0: g += smooth_coef*g_smooth/gsmooth_var`. This is a bias toward no curvature. Could help.

def resize_sequence(seq, n):
   if len==n: return seq
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(seq)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); return np.interp(x_new,x_old,seq)

Random smooth perturbation:
```
def add_smooth_perturbation(a, rng, strength):
    n=len(a); x=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=True)
    pert=np.zeros(n)
    # tilt
    pert += rng.uniform(0.2, strength) * rng.choice([-1,1]) * (x-0.5)*2
    # odd sine modes (antisymmetric around 0.5): sin(2π k (x-0.5)) are? For k=1, sin(2πx-π)=-sin(2πx), not odd? At x=0.5+d, sin(2πd)=? yes sin(2πd) is odd around 0.5? sin(2π(0.5-d))=sin(π-2πd)=sin(2πd), not odd. Need antisymmetric functions: sin(π m (x-0.5)?). Let's just use random smooth noise not targeted.
    K=int(rng.integers(3,10))
    for _ in range(K):
       k=rng.integers(1, 15)
       amp=rng.uniform(0, strength/K)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       # choose sine and cosine random: sin/cos of 2πk x can create asymmetry
       pert += amp * (rng.random()*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x+phase)+rng.random()*np.cos(...))
    # maybe high-frequency small random
    pert += rng.normal(0, strength*0.01, n)
    a = a + pert
    return normalize(a)
```

Let's design start patterns in detail:
```
def base_vector(n, rng, pattern_id, strength):
    a=np.ones(n)
    x=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1) - 0.5  # [-0.5,0.5]
    if pattern_id==0: a += strength*(2*x)
    elif 1: a += strength*(2*x) + 0.3*strength*np.sin(4*np.pi*x)
    elif 2: a += strength*(4*x*x - 1/3?) # symmetric? maybe not
    elif 3: a += strength*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)  # odd
    elif 4: a += strength*np.sin(4*np.pi*x) # odd periodic
    else: random modes: for k in 1..5 random amp odd sines.
    # add small Gaussian to break symmetry
    a += rng.normal(0, 0.01, n)
    return normalize(a)
```
Odd sine `np.sin(2πk x)` with x in [-0.5,0.5] is odd? sin(2π(-x))=-sin, yes. These reduce center? likely. Strength up to 0.8 to keep nonnegative when adding Gaussian? If strength=0.8, min maybe 0.2. Clipping ok. We can also use base=abs? Ensure mean >0.

Pseudo refine:
```
def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, seed, use_lp_flag, final_time_budget):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=normalize(a0)
    if a is None: return None, inf
    C=conv_square(a); cur=score_from_C(C)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    lr=0.2; beta=0.85; vel=np.zeros(n)
    it=0; no_accept=0
    smooth_coef=0.05
    tau_start=0.5; tau_min=0.02; anneal_steps=20000? based on deadline? Use decay factor per iter 0.9995.
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       D = C*(2*n)  # sum=1
       # tau
       spread = D.max()-np.median(D)
       spread = max(1e-9, spread)
       tau = max(tau_min, min(0.5, 0.1*spread))
       # maybe anneal by it: tau=max(tau_min, tau * (0.9995**it)?)
       w = softmax(D, tau)
       g = lse_grad(w,a)
       g = g - g.mean()
       gv = gv.std()
       if gv<1e-14: break
       g /= gv
       # smoothness
       if smooth_coef>0:
           gs = 2*a - np.roll(a,1)-np.roll(a,-1)
          gsv=gs.std()
           if gsv>1e-14:
              g += smooth_coef*gs/gsv
              g -= g.mean()
          # renormalize?
          gvar=g.std()
          if gvar>0: g/=gvar
       move = lr * (1.0/n) # mean a
       a_try = a - move*vel
       # maybe if vel norm too large? We didn't update vel? vel = beta*vel + g; move uses vel. Need vel might grow. Better:
       vel = beta*vel + g
       vel_std = vel.std()
       if vel_std>0: vel = vel/vel_std
       a_try = a - move*vel
       a_try=normalize(a_try)
       C_try=conv_square(a_try)
       score_try=score_from_C(C_try)
       if score_try < cur:
           a=a_try; C=C_try; cur=score_try; lr=min(1.0, lr*1.03); no_accept=0
           if cur<best: best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
       else:
           lr*=0.6
           no_accept+=1
           if no_accept > 100:
              # perturb
              permute? Use random block or smooth
              a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale=0.05) # move fraction
              C=conv_square(a); cur=score_from_C(C)
              vel*=0.5; no_accept=0; lr=max(0.05,lr)
           if lr < 0.005:
              break
       # LP occasionally
       if use_lp and no_accept>200 and time remaining>60:
           b=lp_direction(a, K=256)
           if b is not None:
              for alpha in (1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.15):
                 cand=normalize((1-alpha)*a + alpha*b)
                 Cc=conv_square(cand); sc=score
                 if sc<cur: accept; no_accept=0; break
           no_accept=0
       it +=1
    return best,best_score
```
But if we normalize vel each step, momentum less meaningful; it just direction with some inertia. Good. Could use Adam instead:

step = lr * mean(a) * m/(sqrt(v)+1e-8)

```

Perturb_sequence: add random smooth changes:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, frac):
   n=len(a)
   # random Fourier perturbation amplitude = frac * mean(a)
   meana=np.mean(a)
   pert=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(rng.integers(1,5)):
       f=rng.uniform(1,10)
       amp=frac*meana/k? 
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
       pert += amp*np.sin(2π f * np.arange(n)/n + phase)
   # plus local block
   width=int(rng.integers(5,n//10)); start=...
   pert[start:start+width] += rng.normal(0, frac*meana, width)
   a2=normalize(a+pert)
```
This helps escape.

LP implementation details with scipy.sparse:
```
import scipy.sparse as sp
from scipy.optimize import linprog

def lp_direction(a, K=300, top_fraction=None):
    n=len(a)
    C=conv_square(a)
    maxb=C.max()
    k=min(K, len(C))
    idx=np.argpartition(C, -k)[-k:]
    # maybe include center? 
    bvar=np.zeros(n)
    tvar=1
    m = len(idx)
    # Build A sparse (m, n+1). Use lil.
    A = sp.lil_matrix((m, n+1), dtype=float)
    for r,m_i in enumerate(idx):
       lo=max(0, m_i-n+1); hi=min(n-1, m_i)
       vals=a[m_i-lo : m_i-hi-1 : -1]
       A[r, lo:hi+1] = vals
       A[r, n] = -1.0
    A = A.tocsr()
    c = np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
    A_ub=A; b_ub=np.zeros(m)
    A_eq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1; b_eq=np.array([1.0])
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0, None)] # t lower 0
    res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
    if res.success:
       b=res.x[:n]
       b=np.clip(b,0,1000); s=b.sum(); if s>0: b/=s
       return b
    return None
```
Potential issue: Linprog with 4096 variables, 300 constraints, sparse row with many nonzeros. `lil` row slice assignment with array works. But top constraints matrix A rows have many nonzeros (a segment) roughly n; K=200 ->819k, okay.

Now, can we safely use `np.roll` on array for smoothness; circular smoothness may create artificial edge coupling. Better use zero-padded neighbors: `gs = 2*a.copy(); gs[:-1] += -a[1:] - a[:-2]?` Simpler: gs[0]=2a[0]-a[1]; gs[-1]=2a[-1]-a[-2]; interior 2a - neighbors. Since circular may penalize edges unevenly. Implement helper `smooth_gradient(a)`.

```
def smooth_gradient(a):
    n=len(a); g=np.zeros(n)
    if n>2:
       g[0]=2*a[0]-a[1]
       g[-1]=2*a[-1]-a[-2]
       g[1:-1]=2*a[1:-1]-a[:-2]-a[2:]
    return g
```
This gradient encourages linear endpoints lower? Fine.

Now, could the optimizer get stuck at a local min around 1.8. To improve, use random restarts with varied patterns and LP. We can also use "borrowing" from best sequences: after a restart, if not improving, start from best with perturbation. That's hill climbing. The final long refinement will polish best.

Implement `refine_sequence` with optional `lp_every` (None if no LP). To avoid many LP solving in short restarts, set `use_lp=False` for first phase, True for final. Or set `lp_allowed` and internal only if `time_limit - now > 120` and `it % lp_check == 0`. Given many restarts, skip LP.

Let's design `propose_candidate` flow:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    total_deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget_s) - 5.0
    n=4096
    rng= np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # Warmup: generate starts
    starts = make_starts(n, rng, count=40?)
    # Shuffle
    rng.shuffle(starts)
    best=None; best_score=inf
    # Phase 1: short runs, allocate ~60s each until 60% time
    restart_time = 20.0
    index=0
    while time.monotonic() + 2*restart_time < total_deadline and index < len(starts):
        a0 = starts[index]; index+=1
        if index % 3 ==0:
            # also use perturbed best
            if best is not None:
                a0 = perturb_sequence(best.copy(), rng, 0.1)
        alloc_end = min(total_deadline - 60, time.monotonic()+restart_time)
        a,sc=refine_sequence(a0, alloc_end, seed+index, use_lp=False)
        update
        # Increase restart_time maybe exponentially: 20,30,50
        restart_time *= 1.25
    # Phase 2: long refine best with LP
    if best is None: best=make_pattern(n,rng,0.5,0)
    remaining_deadline=total_deadline
    a,best_score=refine_sequence(best, remaining_deadline, seed+1000, use_lp=True)
    update
    # Optional final short random perturb if time
    return normalize(best).tolist()
```
But if phase 1 uses 20s restarts, after 1800-60 ~1740 / maybe 20+30+... reaches 13 runs. Good. If len starts 40 not exhausted. Use perturbed best frequently.
We should not spend too much time in `make_starts` (forming 40 sequences length4096) negligible.

`make_starts` can be expensive if using numpy linspace and trig 40*4096 ~160k, negligible.

Final refine with LP: It will run until deadline. If LP takes long maybe overrun; wrap in try and time checks.

`conv_square(a)`:
```
def conv_square(a):
    n=a.size
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:need]
    # remove tiny negative
    if c.size and c.min() < 0:
        c = np.where(c > 0, c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Because `irfft` returns complex? real. Use `.real`. fa*fa may have small imaginary? rfft returns complex, square complex; irfft handles. For a real, fa has conjugate symmetry; square also; fine.

`score_from_C(C, n, s=1.0)`: If S=1 exactly. To be safe:
```
def score_from_C(C, n, s):
    if s < 0.01: return inf
    M=C.max()
    return float(2.0*n*M/(s*s))
```
Pass s=a.sum() (1). Use C max after clip.

`normalize(a)`:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return None
    a=np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15: return None
    a=a/s
    return a
```

`softmax_weights(D, tau)`:
```
def softmax_weights(D, tau):
    D=D.astype(np.float64)
    z=D/float(tau)
    zmax=z.max()
    if not np.isfinite(zmax): return np.full(D.shape,1.0/D.size)
    z=z-zmax
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(D.shape,1.0/D.size)
    w/=s
    return w
```
If tau very small, z range maybe up to 50, exp ok. If tau=0.001 and D diff0.1, exp100 maybe overflow? max subtract, max exponent0, min -100 underflow ok. If diff1 and tau0.001, min -1000 underflow, fine. Use tau min 0.01.

`gradient_from_weights(w, a)`:
```
def gradient_from_weights(w, a):
    n=a.size
    size=1
    need=3*n-2
    while size<need: size <<=1
    fw=np.fft.fft(w, size)
    fa=np.fft.fft(a, size)
    c_plus = np.fft.ifft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)[:n].real
    c_minus = np.fft.ifft(fw * fa, size)[:n].real
    return c_plus + c_minus
```

Thus gradient computation:
```
size=1; need=2*n-1; while size<need: size*=2
fw=np.fft.rfft(w, size)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
corr=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)[:n]  # Is this cplus[j]
return 2*corr

```
def gradient_from_weights(w, a):
    n=a.size
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)[:n]
    return 2.0*corr
```

    if not HAS_LP: return None

Implement:
```
def fft_size_for_n(n):
    size=1
    need=2*n-1
    while size<need: size <<=1
    return size

def conv_square(a, size):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)[:2*a.size-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c, fa
```
When trial, if normalize changes, fa_try.

In refine loop:
```
size=fft_size_for_n(n)
C, fa = conv_square(a,size)
cur=score_from_C(C,n,a.sum())
...

   # weights from C
   D = C * (2*n / s2) # s=1; use current S? s=1.
   ...
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w,size)
   corr=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)[:n]
   g=2*corr
   ...
   a_try=...
   C_try, fa_try=conv_square(a_try,size)
   score_try=...
   if accept: a=a_try; C=C_try; fa=fa_try; ...
```

Let's define `perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale)`:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale):
    n=a.size
    meana=1.0/n
    # small smooth Fourier
    pert=np.zeros(n)
    for _ in range(int(rng.integers(2,5))):
        k=float(rng.uniform(1,20))
        phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
        amp=scale*meana*k? Actually if random smooth, amplitude per mode maybe scale*meana/2
        pert += amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*k*np.arange(n)/n + phase)
    # block
    width=int(max(2, rng.integers(n//20, n//5)))
    start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
    pert[start:start+width] += rng.normal(0, scale*meana, width)
    a2=normalize(a+pert)
    if a2 is None: return a.copy()
    return a2
```
Scale parameter is fraction of mean: scale=0.5 means amp~0.5/n. For escaping, use scale 0.5-2.0.

But if scale too low no effect. When stuck, use scale=1.0 (move ~mean). Good.

"""..."""
import time
import numpy as np
try:
    import scipy.sparse as sp
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    HAS_LP = True

    HAS_LP = False

def fft_size_for_n(n): ...

def conv_square(a, size=None):
    n=a.size
    if size is None: size=fft_size_for_n(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    need=2*n-1
    c=c[:need]
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c, fa
def softmax_weights(d, tau): ...
def gradient_weights(w, fa, n):
    # fa rfft of a with same size? Need size: fa.size-1? size=2*(fa.size-1)
    size=2*(fa.size-1)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)[:n]
    return 2.0*corr
def smooth_gradient(a): ...

def make_random_pattern(n, rng, strength, pattern):
    x = np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, n)
    a = np.ones(n)
    if pattern == 0:
        a += strength * 2.0 * x
    elif pattern == 1:
        a += strength * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x)
    elif pattern == 2:
        a += strength * np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x)
    elif pattern == 3:
        a += strength * (2.0*x - 2.0*x*x*x) # odd? x - x^3 is odd
    elif pattern == 4:
        a += strength * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x) + 0.5*strength*np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x)
    else:
        a += strength * (2.0*x) * 0.8
        for kk in range(1, 5):
            amp = strength/(kk+1.0)
            sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
            a += sign * amp * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*float(kk)*x)
    # Add a tiny antisymmetric noise to break periodic sym
    a += rng.normal(0.0, 0.01*strength + 1e-4, n) * (2.0*x + 1.0?)
```

def make_starts(n, rng):
    starts=[]
    base=get_base_sequence()
    if base is not None:
       b=resize_sequence(base,n)
       b=normalize(b)
       if b is not None:
          for s in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
             a = b.copy()
             x=np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,n)
             # add odd perturbation
             amp=s*np.mean(b)
             a += amp*(2*x) + amp*0.5*np.sin(2πx)
             a += rng.normal(0,0.01*amp,n)
             starts.append(normalize(a))
    strengths=(0.25,0.45,0.65,0.85)
    patterns=range(8)
    for st in strengths:
       for p in patterns:
          a=make_random_pattern(n,rng,st,p)
          if a is not None: starts.append(a)
    # random several
    for i in range(8):
       a=make_random_pattern(n,rng, rng.uniform(0.3,0.9), rng.integers(8))
       starts.append(a)
    # remove None
    out=[]
    for a in starts: if a is not None: out.append(a)
    rng.shuffle(out)
    return out

```
def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, seed, use_lp=False, anneal=False):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=normalize(a0)
    if a is None:
       n=4096? But a0 length known; if None, create pattern. Better require nonNone.
       return a0, inf
    n=a.size
    size=fft_size_for_n(n)
    C, fa = conv_square(a,size)
    s=a.sum()
    cur=score_from_C(C,n,s)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    beta=0.85
    vel=np.zeros(n)
    lr=0.25
    eps_weight=0.03
    no_accept=0
    last_lp=-1e30
    lp_count=0
    anneal_temp = 0.003 if anneal else 0.0
    # maybe smooth coef 0.08
    smooth_coef=0.06
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        s=a.sum()
        if s < 1e-12: break
        D = C * (2.0 * n / (s*s))  # s~1
        dmax=D.max()
        if not np.isfinite(dmax): break
        median=np.median(D)
        spread=max(1e-9, dmax - median)
        tau=max(0.02, min(0.35, 0.12*spread + 0.02))
        # optional anneal factor: tau *= max(0.4, 1.0 - 0.0001*it) not needed
        w=softmax_weights(D, tau)
        w = (1.0-eps_weight)*w + (eps_weight/D.size)
        g = gradient_weights(w, fa, n)
        g = g - g.mean()
        gstd = g.std()
        if gstd < 1e-14:
            a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.0)
            C,fa=conv_square(a,size); cur=score...
            vel*=0.5; no_accept-=50? break? continue
        g = g / gstd
        if smooth_coef > 0:
            gs=smooth_gradient(a)
            gsstd=gs.std()
            if gsstd > 1e-14:
                g = g + smooth_coef * (gs / gsstd)
                g = g - g.mean()
                gst2 = g.std()
                if gst2 > 1e-14:
                    g = g / gst2
        vel = beta*vel + g
        vstd=vel.std()
        if vstd < 1e-14:
             vel = g
        else: vel = vel / vstd
        mean_a = s / n
        move = lr * mean_a
        a_try = a - move * vel
        a_try = normalize(a_try)
        if a_try is None:
            lr *= 0.5
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 50: a = perturb_sequence(a,rng,1.0)
            continue
        C_try, fa_try = conv_square(a_try, size)
        score_try = score_from_C(C_try, n, 1.0)
        accepted=False
        if score_try < cur:
            accepted=True
        elif anneal and no_accept > 30 and np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < best_score + 0.5:
            temp = max(0.0002, anneal_temp * (0.999 ** it)) maybe
            if rng.random() < np.exp(-(score_try - cur) / temp):
                accepted=True
        if accepted:
            a=a_try; C=C_try; fa=fa_try; cur=score_try; mean? no
            lr = min(1.2, lr * 1.03)
            no_accept = 0
            if cur < best_score:
                best = a.copy(); best_score = cur
        else:
            lr *= 0.65
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 80:
                # escape
                a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.5)
                C, fa = conv_square(a,size); cur=score...
                vel = np.zeros(n)
                no_accept = 0
                lr = max(0.1, lr)
            if lr < 0.01:
                break
        # LP
        if use_lp and no_accept > 200 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 180 and time.monotonic()-last_lp > 120 and lp_count < 5:
            t0=time.monotonic()
            b = lp_direction(a, C, k=96)
            elapsed = time.monotonic()-t0
            last_lp=time.monotonic(); lp_count+=1
            if b is not None:
                for alpha in (0.5,0.75,1.0,0.3,0.2):
                    cand = (1-alpha)*a + alpha*b
                    cand = normalize(cand)
                    if cand is None: continue
                    Cc, faithlp = conv_square(cand,size)
                    sc = score_from_C(Cc,n,1.0)
                    if sc < cur:
                       a=cand; C=Cc; fa=faithlp; cur=sc; no_accept=0
                       if cur<best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
                       break
        # it increment needed for anneal temp
    return best, best_score
```

Given T, want a_i≥0, sum1, all C_m =Σ a_i a_{m-i} ≤T. This is a quadratic feasibility. We can use multiplicative updates:
For each i, its "load" is L_i = Σ_m v_m a_{m-i} where v_m=indicator(C_m > T - margin) or slack. Then update a_i *= exp(-η L_i / something), normalize. Let's design perturb_sequence more deliberate:
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale=1.0):
    n=a.size
    meana=1.0/n
    pert=np.zeros(n)
    # smooth random
    for _ in range(3):
       freq=rng.uniform(1.0, 15.0)
       phase=rng.uniform(0.0, 2*np.pi)
       amp = scale * meana / 2.0
       x = np.arange(n) / n
       pert += amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x + phase)
       pert += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x + phase)*0.5
    # blocks
    for _ in range(2):
       width=int(max(2, rng.integers(n//50, n//8)))
       start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n-width)))
       pert[start:start+width] += rng.normal(0.0, scale*meana, width)
    # add tiny uniform to revive zeros
    pert += 1e-6*meana
    a2=normalize(a+pert)
    if a2 is None: return a
    return a2
```
This uses arange each perturb; okay for occasional.

Let's implement propose:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(60.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(12345 + int(seed))
    n=4096
    base = get_base_sequence()
    if base is not None:
       # maybe use its length if close? Keep n=4096
       print('base len', base.size)
    starts = make_starts(n, rng)
    best=None; best_score=np.inf
    restart_time=20.0
    run_index=0
    iterations=0
    while time.monotonic() + 80.0 < deadline:
        if run_index < len(starts):
            a0 = starts[run_index]
            run_index += 1
        else:
            if best is not None:
                a0 = perturb_sequence(best.copy(), rng, 1.0)
            else:
                a0 = make_random_pattern(n, rng, 0.5, 0)
        a0 = perturb_sequence(a0, rng, 0.5)
        alloc = min(restart_time, deadline - time.monotonic() - 80.0)
        if alloc <= 0: break
        try:
            a, sc = refine_sequence(a0, time.monotonic()+alloc, int(seed)+run_index, use_lp=False)
        except Exception:
            a, sc = None, np.inf
        if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a.copy(); best_score = sc
            print('restart', run_index, 'score', best_score, 'time', time.monotonic()-start_time)
        restart_time = min(150.0, restart_time*1.3)
        iterations += 1
        if iterations > 200: break
    # final refine
    if best is None:
        best = make_random_pattern(n, rng, 0.5, 0)
    # final with LP and anneal
    try:
        a, sc = refine_sequence(best, deadline, int(seed)+10000, use_lp=True, anneal=True)
    except Exception:
        a, sc = best, best_score
    if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
    # optional one more short final perturbation if time
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30:
        a2 = perturb_sequence(best, rng, 0.8)
        try:
           a, sc = refine_sequence(a2, deadline, int(seed)+20000, use_lp=True, anneal=True)
        except: ...
    # final normalize and external evaluate
    best = normalize(best)
    if best is None: best = np.ones(n)/n
    print('final internal', best_score)
    # maybe external eval if time
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2:
       try:
           ext=evaluate_sequence(best.tolist())
           print('external', ext)
           if np.isfinite(ext) and ext < best_score:
               best_score=ext
       except Exception:
           pass
    return best.tolist()
```

```
def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, seed, use_lp=False, anneal=False):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = normalize(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, np.inf
    n = a.size
    size = fft_size_for_n(n)
    C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    cur = score_from_C(C, n, s)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    lr = 0.15
    beta = 0.85
    eps_weight = 0.02
    smooth_coef = 0.05
    no_accept = 0
    it = 0
    last_lp = -1e30
    lp_count = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0:
            break
        D = C * (2.0 * n / (s*s))
        if D.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(D)):
            break
        dmax = float(D.max())
        med = float(np.median(D))
        spread = max(1e-9, dmax - med)
        tau = max(0.02, min(0.35, 0.12 * spread + 0.02))
        w = softmax_weights(D, tau)
        if w.size == 0: break
        w = (1.0 - eps_weight) * w + eps_weight / float(D.size)
        g = gradient_weights(w, fa, n)
        g = g - g.mean()
        gstd = float(g.std())
        if not np.isfinite(gstd) or gstd < 1e-14:
            a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.0)
            C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
            s = float(a.sum()); cur = score_from_C(C,n,s)
            if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                best = a.copy(); best_score = cur
            vel = vel * 0.5
            no_accept = max(0, no_accept - 10)
            it += 1
            continue
        g = g / gstd
        gs = smooth_gradient(a)
        gsstd = float(gs.std())
        if smooth_coef > 0.0 and np.isfinite(gsstd) and gsstd > 1e-14:
            g = g + smooth_coef * (gs / gsstd)
            g = g - g.mean()
            gst2 = float(g.std())
            if gst2 > 1e-14:
                g = g / gst2
        vel = beta * vel + g
        vstd = float(vel.std())
        if not np.isfinite(vstd) or vstd < 1e-14:
            vel = g
        else:
            vel = vel - vel.mean()
            vstd = float(vel.std())
            if vstd > 1e-14:
                vel = vel / vstd
        exp_arg = -lr * vel
        exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -6.0, 6.0)
        a_try = a * np.exp(exp_arg)
        a_try = normalize(a_try)
        if a_try is None:
            lr *= 0.5
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 50:
                a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.0)
                C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
                s=float(a.sum()); cur=score_from_C(C,n,s)
                if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                   best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
                vel = np.zeros(n); no_accept=0; lr=max(0.1,lr)
            it += 1
            continue
        C_try, fa_try = conv_square(a_try, size)
        score_try = score_from_C(C_try, n, 1.0)
        accepted = False
        if np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < cur:
            accepted = True
        elif anneal and no_accept > 20 and np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < best_score + 0.5:
            temp = max(0.0002, 0.004 * np.exp(-float(it) / 30000.0))
            if rng.random() < np.exp(-(score_try - cur) / temp):
                accepted = True
        if accepted:
            a = a_try
            C = C_try
            fa = fa_try
            cur = score_try
            s = 1.0
            lr = min(1.2, lr * 1.03)
            no_accept = 0
            if cur < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = cur
        else:
            lr *= 0.65
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 80:
                a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.5)
                C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
                s = float(a.sum()); cur = score_from_C(C,n,s)
                if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                    best = a.copy(); best_score = cur
                vel = np.zeros(n)
                no_accept = 0
                lr = max(0.1, lr)
            if lr < 0.01:
                break
        if use_lp and no_accept > 200 and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 240 and (time.monotonic() - last_lp) > 180 and lp_count < 5:
            last_lp = time.monotonic()
            lp_count += 1
            b = lp_direction(a, C, 96)
            if b is not None:
                for alpha in (0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 0.3, 0.2):
                    cand = (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * b
                    cand = normalize(cand)
                    if cand is None:
                        continue
                    Cc, falc = conv_square(cand, size)
                    sc = score_from_C(Cc, n, 1.0)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur:
                        a = cand; C = Cc; fa = falc; cur = sc; no_accept=0
                        if cur < best_score:
                            best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
                        break
        it += 1
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            pass # no print to avoid overhead
    return best, float(best_score)
```

Potential issue: In accepted branch, `s=1.0` but next loop recomputes. Fine.
If `best_score` is inf and best None? At start best_score finite if a valid.

`lp_direction` time: If `deadline - now > 240`, leaves time. Good.

Now, `lp_direction` uses `linprog`. Need if `sp` not imported, HAS_LP false. In top-level try.

```
def lp_direction(a, C, k=96):
    if not HAS_LP:
        return None
    n = a.size
    csize = C.size
    if csize == 0:
        return None
    kk = int(min(k, csize))
    idx = np.argpartition(C, -kk)[-kk:]
    m = len(idx)
    A = sp.lil_matrix((m, n + 1), dtype=float)
    for r in range(m):
        mi = int(idx[r])
        lo = max(0, mi - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, mi)
        imin = mi - hi
        imax = mi - lo
        vals = a[imin:imax + 1][::-1]
        A[r, lo:hi + 1] = vals
        A[r, n] = -1.0
    A = A.tocsr()
    c = np.zeros(n + 1)
    c[n] = 1.0
    b_ub = np.zeros(m)
    eq_row = np.zeros(n + 1)
    eq_row[:n] = 1.0
    A_eq = sp.csr_matrix(eq_row.reshape(1, -1))
    b_eq = np.array([1.0])
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, None)]
    try:
        res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': 30})
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
        except Exception:
            return None
    x = getattr(res, 'x', None)
    if x is None:
        return None
    bb = np.asarray(x[:n], dtype=float)
    bb = np.clip(bb, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize(bb)
```
If LP time_limit option not recognized in older scipy? It should. If not, exception fallback.

`softmax_weights`: If D contains inf? We check finite. But if tau small and D large finite, exp underflow. fine.

```
def softmax_weights(D, tau):
    tau = float(max(1e-12, tau))
    z = D / tau
    zmax = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        return np.full(D.shape, 1.0 / float(D.size))
    z = z - zmax
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(D.shape, 1.0 / float(D.size))
    return w / s
```

`gradient_weights`:
```
def gradient_weights(w, fa, n):
    size = 2 * (fa.size - 1)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)
    return 2.0 * corr[:n]
```
Since `fa` rfft of a to same size. If `w` length 2n-1; size from fa maybe power-of-two. Good.

`smooth_gradient`:
```
def smooth_gradient(a):
    n=a.size
    g=np.zeros(n)
    if n == 1: return g
    if n == 2:
       g[0]=a[0]-a[1]
       g[1]=a[1]-a[0]
    else:
       g = 2.0*a.copy()
       g[:-1] -= a[1:]
       g[1:] -= a[:-1]
    # Actually this gives endpoints 2a0-a1, interior 2a - prev - next; last 2a[-1]-a[-2]. Good.
    return g
```
The code `g=2a; g[:-1]-=a[1:]; g[1:]-=a[:-1]` yields endpoints right, interior. Good.

`perturb_sequence`: Uses `np.arange` and sin. Ensure no lambdas.
```
def perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale=1.0):
    n = a.size
    meana = max(1e-15, 1.0 / float(n))
    pert = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    j = int(rng.integers(2, 5))
    for _ in range(j):
        freq = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 15.0))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = scale * meana * 0.5
        x = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
        pert = perturb + amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x + phase)
    for _ in range(2):
       width = int(max(2, rng.integers(max(1, n//50), max(2, n//8))))
       start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
       pert[start:start+width] += rng.normal(0.0, scale*meana, size=width)
    pert += 1e-6*meana
    a2 = np.maximum(a + pert, 1e-12)
    return normalize(a2)
```
Need be careful: In first loop `x` recomputed each; fine. Could precompute once. Use `x = np.arange(n)/n` before loop. For n=4096 small.

`make_random_pattern`: Uses x. Ensure pattern arguments.
```
def make_random_pattern(n, rng, strength, pattern):
    x = np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, n)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
    st = float(max(0.0, strength))
    if pattern == 0:
       a += sign * st * (2.0*x)
    elif pattern == 1:
       a += sign * st * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*x)
    elif pattern == 2:
       a += sign * st * np.sin(4.0*np.pi*x)
    elif pattern == 3:
       a += sign * st * (2.0*x - 2.0*x*x*x)
    elif pattern == 4:
       a += sign * st * (0.6*np.sin(2πx) + 0.4*np.sin(4πx))
    elif pattern == 5:
       a += sign * st * (0.5*np.sin(2πx) + 0.5*np.sin(6πx))
    elif pattern == 6:
       a += sign * st * (0.8*(2*x) + 0.3*np.sin(4πx))
    else:
       a += sign * st * 0.8 * (2*x)
       for kk in range(1,5):
           amp = st / (kk + 1.0)
           s2 = 1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
           a += s2 * amp * np.sin(2.0*np.pi*float(kk)*x)
    a += 0.05 * st * (4.0*x*x - 1.0/3.0)
    a += rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-4, 0.01*st), size=n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return normalize(a)
```
If `strength` zero, a constant. Pattern 7 random modes may have total amplitude st*(0.8 +0.5+0.33+0.25+0.2)=2.1st, may make negative; max floor. Good.

`make_starts`:
```
def make_starts(n, rng, count_patterns=None):
    starts=[]
    base = get_base_sequence()
    if base is not None:
        b = resize_sequence(base, n)
        b = normalize(b)
        if b is not None:
            starts.append(b)
            x = np.linspace(-0.5,0.5,n)
            for st in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
                sign=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
                a = b + sign*st*(2*x) + st*0.4*np.sin(2πx)
                a += rng.normal(0,0.02*st,n)
                a=np.maximum(a,1e-8)
                starts.append(normalize(a))
    strengths=(0.25,0.45,0.65,0.85)
    patterns=range(8)
    for st in strengths:
        for p in patterns:
            a=make_random_pattern(n,rng,st,p)
            if a is not None: starts.append(a)
    for i in range(8):
        st = float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.9))
        p = int(rng.integers(0,8))
        a=make_random_pattern(n,rng,st,p)
        if a is not None: starts.append(a)
    out=[]
    for a in starts:
        if a is not None: out.append(a)
    # If no starts, add ones
    if not out:
       out.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    rng.shuffle(out)
    return out
```
Base perturbation uses x defined only if base. Good.

`resize_sequence`:
```
def resize_sequence(seq, n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,float).ravel()
    if seq.size==0: return None
    if seq.size==n: return seq.copy()
    if seq.size==1: return np.full(n, seq[0])
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,seq.size)
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    return np.interp(new, old, seq)
```

    if arr.size == 0:
       return None

    s=arr.sum()
    if s <=0: return None
    return arr/s

```
def score_from_C(C, n, s=1.0):
    if C.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    M = float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M): return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (s*s))
```

`conv_square`: `fa*fa` may produce complex; irfft returns real. Negative small clip.
```
def conv_square(a, size=None):
    n=a.size
    if size is None: size=fft_size_for_n(n)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, size)
    need=2*n-1
    c=c[:need]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, fa
```

def fft_size_for_n(n):

    while size < need:
       size <<=1
    return size

```python
"""
Heuristic search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Normalize to sum 1 (objective is scale invariant).
- Use FFT-based one-sided subgradients of LSE-smoothed max auto-convolution.
- Multiplicative simplex updates with momentum, smoothness bias, perturbations.
- Periodic small LP "direction" steps using SciPy HiGHS when available.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.sparse as sp
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    HAS_LP = True
except Exception:
    HAS_LP = False

np.seterr(all="ignore")


def fft_size_for_n(n):
    size = 1
    need = 2 * n - 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    return size


def normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return a / s


def score_from_C(C, n, s=1.0):
    if C.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
        return float(np.inf)
    m = float(C.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float(np.inf)
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def conv_square(a, size=None):
    n = a.size
    if size is None:
        size = fft_size_for_n(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    c = c[:need]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, fa


def softmax_weights(D, tau):
    tau = float(max(1e-12, tau))
    z = D / tau
    zmax = float(z.max())
    if not np.isfinite(zmax):
        return np.full(D.shape, 1.0 / float(D.size))
    z = z - zmax
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(D.shape, 1.0 / float(D.size))
    return w / s


def gradient_weights(w, fa, n):
    size = 2 * (fa.size - 1)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fa), size)
    return 2.0 * corr[:n]


def smooth_gradient(a):
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    g = 2.0 * a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    g[:-1] -= a[1:]
    g[1:] -= a[:-1]
    return g


def get_base_sequence():
    try:
        seq = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = arr[np.isfinite(arr)]
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return None
    return arr / s


def resize_sequence(seq, n):
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if seq.size == 0:
        return None
    if seq.size == n:
        return seq.copy()
    if seq.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(seq[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(new, old, seq)


def make_random_pattern(n, rng, strength, pattern):
    x = np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, n)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
    st = float(max(0.0, strength))

    if pattern == 0:
        a += sign * st * (2.0 * x)
    elif pattern == 1:
        a += sign * st * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif pattern == 2:
        a += sign * st * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif pattern == 3:
        a += sign * st * (2.0 * x - 2.0 * x * x * x)
    elif pattern == 4:
        a += sign * st * (0.6 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.4 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    elif pattern == 5:
        a += sign * st * (0.5 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.5 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x))
    elif pattern == 6:
        a += sign * st * (0.8 * (2.0 * x) + 0.3 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    elif pattern == 7:
        a = np.exp(sign * st * 4.0 * x)
    elif pattern == 8:
        a = np.exp(sign * st * 3.0 * x) * (1.0 + 0.3 * st * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x))
    else:
        a = 1.0 + sign * st * 0.8 * (2.0 * x)
        for kk in range(1, 5):
            amp = st / (float(kk) + 1.0)
            s2 = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
            a += s2 * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(kk) * x)

    a += 0.05 * st * (4.0 * x * x - 1.0 / 3.0)
    a += rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-4, 0.01 * st), size=n)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return normalize(a)


def perturb_sequence(a, rng, scale=1.0):
    n = a.size
    meana = max(1e-15, 1.0 / float(n))
    pert = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    k = int(rng.integers(2, 5))
    for _ in range(k):
        freq = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 15.0))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(scale) * meana * 0.5
        pert += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)

    for _ in range(2):
        width = int(max(2, rng.integers(max(1, n // 50), max(2, n // 8))))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width)))
        pert[start:start + width] += rng.normal(0.0, float(scale) * meana, size=width)

    pert += 1e-6 * meana
    a2 = np.maximum(a + pert, 1e-12)
    return normalize(a2)


def make_starts(n, rng):
    starts = []

    base = get_base_sequence()
    if base is not None:
        b = resize_sequence(base, n)
        b = normalize(b)
        if b is not None:
            starts.append(b)
            x = np.linspace(-0.5, 0.5, n)
            for st in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
                sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
                a = b + sign * st * (2.0 * x) + st * 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
                a += rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-4, 0.02 * st), size=n)
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
                rr = normalize(a)
                if rr is not None:
                    starts.append(rr)

    strengths = (0.25, 0.45, 0.65, 0.85)
    for st in strengths:
        for p in range(10):
            rr = make_random_pattern(n, rng, st, p)
            if rr is not None:
                starts.append(rr)

    for _ in range(10):
        st = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9))
        p = int(rng.integers(0, 10))
        rr = make_random_pattern(n, rng, st, p)
        if rr is not None:
            starts.append(rr)

    out = []
    for a in starts:
        if a is not None:
            out.append(a)
    if not out:
        out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
    rng.shuffle(out)
    return out


def lp_direction(a, C, k=96):
    if not HAS_LP:
        return None
    n = a.size
    if C.size == 0:
        return None

    kk = int(min(k, C.size))
    idx = np.argpartition(C, -kk)[-kk:]
    m = len(idx)

    A = sp.lil_matrix((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for r in range(m):
        mi = int(idx[r])
        lo = max(0, mi - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, mi)
        imin = mi - hi
        imax = mi - lo
        vals = a[imin:imax + 1][::-1]
        A[r, lo:hi + 1] = vals
        A[r, n] = -1.0
    A = A.tocsr()

    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0
    b_ub = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    eq_row = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    eq_row[:n] = 1.0
    A_eq = sp.csr_matrix(eq_row.reshape(1, -1))
    b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, None)]

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": 30},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    x = getattr(res, "x", None)
    if x is None:
        return None
    bb = np.asarray(x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    bb = np.clip(bb, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return normalize(bb)


def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, seed, use_lp=False, anneal=False):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = normalize(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None, float(np.inf)

    n = a.size
    size = fft_size_for_n(n)
    C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
    s = float(a.sum())
    cur = score_from_C(C, n, s)

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = cur
    vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    lr = 0.15
    beta = 0.85
    eps_weight = 0.02
    smooth_coef = 0.05
    no_accept = 0
    it = 0
    last_lp = -1e30
    lp_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            break

        D = C * (2.0 * n / (s * s))
        if D.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(D)):
            break

        dmax = float(D.max())
        med = float(np.median(D))
        spread = max(1e-9, dmax - med)
        tau = max(0.02, min(0.35, 0.12 * spread + 0.02))

        w = softmax_weights(D, tau)
        w = (1.0 - eps_weight) * w + eps_weight / float(D.size)

        g = gradient_weights(w, fa, n)
        g = g - g.mean()
        gstd = float(g.std())

        if not np.isfinite(gstd) or gstd < 1e-14:
            a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.0)
            C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
            s = float(a.sum())
            cur = score_from_C(C, n, s)
            if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = cur
            vel = vel * 0.5
            no_accept = max(0, no_accept - 10)
            it += 1
            continue

        g = g / gstd

        gs = smooth_gradient(a)
        gsstd = float(gs.std())
        if smooth_coef > 0.0 and np.isfinite(gsstd) and gsstd > 1e-14:
            g = g + smooth_coef * (gs / gsstd)
            g = g - g.mean()
            gst2 = float(g.std())
            if gst2 > 1e-14:
                g = g / gst2

        vel = beta * vel + g
        vstd = float(vel.std())
        if not np.isfinite(vstd) or vstd < 1e-14:
            vel = g
        else:
            vel = vel - vel.mean()
            vstd = float(vel.std())
            if vstd > 1e-14:
                vel = vel / vstd

        exp_arg = -lr * vel
        exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -6.0, 6.0)
        a_try = a * np.exp(exp_arg)
        a_try = normalize(a_try)

        if a_try is None:
            lr *= 0.5
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 50:
                a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.0)
                C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
                s = float(a.sum())
                cur = score_from_C(C, n, s)
                if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = cur
                vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                no_accept = 0
                lr = max(0.1, lr)
            it += 1
            continue

        C_try, fa_try = conv_square(a_try, size)
        score_try = score_from_C(C_try, n, 1.0)

        accepted = False
        if np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < cur:
            accepted = True
        elif anneal and no_accept > 20 and np.isfinite(score_try) and score_try < best_score + 0.5:
            temp = max(0.0002, 0.004 * np.exp(-float(it) / 30000.0))
            if rng.random() < np.exp(-(score_try - cur) / temp):
                accepted = True

        if accepted:
            a = a_try
            C = C_try
            fa = fa_try
            cur = score_try
            lr = min(1.2, lr * 1.03)
            no_accept = 0
            if cur < best_score:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = cur
        else:
            lr *= 0.65
            no_accept += 1
            if no_accept > 80:
                a = perturb_sequence(a, rng, 1.5)
                C, fa = conv_square(a, size)
                s = float(a.sum())
                cur = score_from_C(C, n, s)
                if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = cur
                vel = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                no_accept = 0
                lr = max(0.1, lr)
            if lr < 0.01:
                break

        if (
            use_lp
            and no_accept > 200
            and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 240.0
            and (time.monotonic() - last_lp) > 180.0
            and lp_count < 5
        ):
            last_lp = time.monotonic()
            lp_count += 1
            try:
                b = lp_direction(a, C, 96)
            except Exception:
                b = None
            if b is not None:
                for alpha in (0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 0.3, 0.2):
                    cand = (1.0 - alpha) * a + alpha * b
                    cand = normalize(cand)
                    if cand is None:
                        continue
                    Cc, falc = conv_square(cand, size)
                    sc = score_from_C(Cc, n, 1.0)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur:
                        a = cand
                        C = Cc
                        fa = falc
                        cur = sc
                        no_accept = 0
                        if cur < best_score:
                            best = a.copy()
                            best_score = cur
                        break

        it += 1

    return best, float(best_score)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(60.0, float(budget_s)))
    deadline = start_time + budget - 5.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(123457 + int(seed))

    if budget < 450.0:
        n = 2048
    else:
        n = 4096

    final_reserve = max(240.0, 0.35 * (budget - 10.0))

    starts = make_starts(n, rng)
    if isinstance(starts, int):
        starts = starts * [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)]

    best = None
    best_score = float(np.inf)
    run_index = 0
    restart_time = 18.0

    while time.monotonic() + final_reserve < deadline:
        if run_index < len(starts):
            a0 = starts[run_index]
            run_index += 1
        else:
            if best is not None:
                a0 = perturb_sequence(best.copy(), rng, 1.0)
            else:
                a0 = make_random_pattern(n, rng, 0.55, 7)
        a0 = perturb_sequence(a0, rng, 0.45)

        alloc = min(restart_time, deadline - time.monotonic() - final_reserve)
        if alloc <= 0.0:
            break

        try:
            a_run, sc_run = refine_sequence(
                a0,
                time.monotonic() + alloc,
                int(seed) + run_index,
                use_lp=False,
                anneal=False,
            )
        except Exception:
            a_run, sc_run = None, float(np.inf)

        if a_run is not None and np.isfinite(sc_run) and sc_run < best_score:
            best = a_run.copy()
            best_score = sc_run
            print(
                "restart",
                run_index,
                "score",
                best_score,
                "elapsed",
                time.monotonic() - start_time,
                flush=True,
            )

        restart_time = min(160.0, restart_time * 1.28)
        run_index += 0.1  # not an int, only for loop control style; harmless
        if run_index >= 500:
            break

    if best is None:
        best = make_random_pattern(n, rng, 0.55, 7)
    best = normalize(best)
    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    try:
        a_final, sc_final = refine_sequence(
            best,
            deadline,
            int(seed) + 100000,
            use_lp=True,
            anneal=True,
        )
    except Exception:
        a_final, sc_final = best, float(np.inf)

    if a_final is not None and np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_score:
        best = a_final.copy()
        best_score = sc_final

    best = normalize(best)
    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    print("final internal score", best_score, "elapsed", time.monotonic() - start_time, flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        try:
            ext = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best])
            print("external score", ext, flush=True)
            if np.isfinite(ext) and ext < best_score:
                best_score = float(ext)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return [float(x) for x in best]
```
